Boats arriving from T py fll inward dues if discharging at Shasi town, local trade dues if dish near the West station; but in table they are all entered as local traders. In the same way boats Shasi town to Tangyang pay full ont us but are classed among local elearances. Boats arriving from Tangyang for other ports pany full outward dues. They are entered in the Shipping fable Airst as local entries, then as interport clearances. Boats arriving from other ports for Tangyang have usualy no dues to pay, but in the 81npping table they are entered, firstly, as interport entries and, seconldly, as local elearances. PART 11L NOTIFICATIONS, During the Quarter ended March 81, 1911. 18
74 MTSTOMS GAZETTE. JAN.-MAT., NOTICES TO MARINERS. CHINA SEA. No. 500. CANTON DISTRICT. BAR ISLAND LIGHT ESTABLISHED. NoIcz is hereby given that Bar Island Light was exhibited for the first time at sunset on the ist February. The illuminating apparatus is dioptric, group-occulting, of the sixth order, showing a fixed white light varied by two eclipses every 16 seconds, thus:- Light . . . . . . . . . . . . io seconds, Eclipse..... .......... 2 Light...... ............ 2 Eclipse ... . . . . . . . . 2 ,, and so on. The light, which is on the western shore of First Bar Island, is hoisted on a mast, is elevated 40 feet above high water, and should be visible in clear weather at a distarice of io nautical miles. SHANGHAI, 3rd February 19 I I. No. 501. YANGTZE RIVER-CHANG DISTRICT. UNCHARTED ROCK NEAR PIHYANG. NoTICE is hereby given of the existence of an uncharted rock 45 yards off the left bank of the river and about j mile above Pihyang. The rock is covered 6 feet at low river (Ichang zero). SHANGHAI, 20th February 191 I.
1911] NOTIFICATIONS. 275 No. 502. SHANGHAI DISTRICT. NORTH CHANNEL ENTRANCE TO THE YANGTZE. DEPTH ON TSUNGMING CROSSING. NoTICE is hereby given that the depths on the Tsungming Crossing have deepened, and that on the Bar in the vicinity of the Second Crossing Buoy there is now a least depth of x5 feet of water at low water of spring tides. SHANGHAI, 25th February 1911. No. 503. NEW ISSUE OF STORM SIGNAL CODE. NOTICE is hereby given that a new Storm Signal Code, in connexion with Storm Warnings issued by Sikawei Observatory, has been issued for use at the Storm Signal Stations of the Imperial Maritime Customs at Newchwang, Taku, Chefoo, Chinkiang, Woosung, Gutzlaff, Pagoda Anchorage, Amoy, and Swatow. The new code comes into use on the ist March, and is identical with the existing code except in respect to additional signals. Copies of the new code can be obtained at the Coast Inspector&#39;s office or at the Municipal Storm Signal Station at Shanghai. SHANGHAI, 28th February 1911. No. 504. FOOCHOW DISTRICT. ENTRANCE TO PAGODA ANCHORAGE. NOTICE is hereby given that the channel in the neighbourhood of Spiteful Island, at the entrance to Pagoda Anchorage, is rapidly changing. The channel is now marked by a black buoy on the western extremity of the Niutau Rocks. The temporary buoys recently placed in position have been removed. The available depth in the channel on the 14th instant was 9 feet at low water of spring tides. SHANGHAI, 28th February 1911.
CUSTOMS GAZETTE. [JAN.-MAR., 19ii. No. 505. SHANGHAI DISTRICT. APPROACH TO WOOSUNG RIVER-QUARANTINE BEACON SHIFTED. NOTICE is hereby given that, owing to the washing away of the western end of Chung- paosha, the Quarantine Beacon has been shifted. In its new position, Woosung Lighthouse bears S. 41&#39; 47&#39; W. (magnetic), distant 3.39 miles. SHANGHAI, 2nd March 1911. No. 506. SHANGHAI DISTRICT. ALTERATION IN BUOYAGE. NOTICE is hereby given that, on or about the 8th instant, the Blockhouse Buoy will be shifted I.6 cables S. 25* W. from its present position, and the Quarantine Lower Buoy will be shifted 2.5 cables S. 66&#39; W. from its present position. All bearings given are magnetic. SHANGHAI, 4th March 1911. No. 507. SHANGHAI DISTRICT. ALTERATIONS IN BUOYAGE. REFERRING to Notice to Mariners No. 506, notice is hereby given that the Blockhouse Buoy and the Quarantine Lower Buoy have been shifted to the following positions:- The Blockhouse Buoy is now moored in 35 feet at low water of spring tides. From the buoy, Blockhouse Island Beacon bears S. j710 E., distant 4.06 miles. The Quarantine Lower Buoy is now moored in 38 feet at low water of spring tides. From the buoy, Woosung Lighthouse bears S. 6 1 W., distant 2.22 miles. All bearings given are magnetie. SHANGHAI, 13th March 19 11.
